// ===================== МОДЕЛИ =====================

data class Word(
    val key: String, val level: String, val word: String, val pos: String, val ipa: String,
    val us: String, val tr: List<String>, val example: String, val exampleRu: String, val emoji: String
)

data class Prog(val s: Int, val due: Long, val mastered: Boolean, val intro: Long, val lapses: Int, val lastWrong: Long)
data class Quiz(val text: String, val options: List<String>, val answer: Int)
data class GUnit(
    val id: String, val level: String, val title: String, val rule: String, val formula: String,
    val examples: List<String>, val mistake: String, val quiz: List<Quiz>
)
data class TextItem(val id: String, val level: String, val title: String, val body: String, val questions: List<Quiz>)
data class Term(val title: String, val explain: String, val example: String, val wiki: String)
data class Q(
    val section: String, val level: String, val text: String, val options: List<String>, val answer: Int,
    val tip: String, val wordKey: String?, val unitId: String?, val passage: String? = null
)
data class TestRec(val day: Long, val kind: String, val level: String, val percent: Int, val passed: Boolean)

class Res(val ok: Boolean, val fixed: String?, val info: String)
class Medal(val title: String, val desc: String, val done: Boolean, val frac: Float, val label: String)
class TestResult(
    val percent: Int, val passed: Boolean, val newLevel: String?,
    val sections: List<Pair<String, Int>>, val wrong: List<Pair<Q, Int>>
)

class TestSession(val kind: String, val target: String, val qs: List<Q>, val limitMs: Long) {
    val startedAt = System.currentTimeMillis()
    var index by mutableStateOf(0)
    val answers = mutableStateListOf<Int>().also { l -> repeat(qs.size) { l.add(-1) } }
    var result by mutableStateOf<TestResult?>(null)
}

// ===================== КОНТЕНТ =====================

private val UNITS = listOf(
    GUnit(
        "be", "A0", "Глагол to be: am / is / are",
        "Глагол to be значит «быть». В настоящем времени у него три формы: am, is, are. Какую форму взять, зависит от того, кто действует. После I ставим am, после he, she, it ставим is, после we, you, they ставим are.",
        "I am • he / she / it is • we / you / they are",
        listOf("I am a student. — Я студент.", "She is at home. — Она дома.", "They are friends. — Они друзья."),
        "Нельзя говорить «I is» или «he are». Форма должна подходить тому, кто действует: I am, he is.",
        listOf(
            Quiz("She ___ a teacher.", listOf("am", "is", "are", "be"), 1),
            Quiz("We ___ at school.", listOf("is", "am", "are", "be"), 2),
            Quiz("I ___ happy today.", listOf("am", "is", "are", "be"), 0),
            Quiz("They ___ my friends.", listOf("is", "are", "am", "be"), 1),
            Quiz("It ___ a big house.", listOf("are", "am", "be", "is"), 3)
        )
    ),
    GUnit(
        "present-simple", "A1", "Present Simple: я работаю, он работает",
        "Present Simple рассказывает о привычках и фактах. С I, you, we, they глагол не меняется: I work. С he, she, it к глаголу добавляем -s: he works. Для вопроса и отрицания нужны do или does, а после does глагол снова без -s.",
        "I / you / we / they + глагол • he / she / it + глагол-s",
        listOf("I drink tea every day. — Я пью чай каждый день.", "She works in a bank. — Она работает в банке.", "Does he like coffee? — Он любит кофе?"),
        "Часто забывают -s: «he work». И наоборот, ставят -s после does: «does he works». После does глагол без -s.",
        listOf(
            Quiz("He ___ football on Sundays.", listOf("play", "plays", "playing", "is play"), 1),
            Quiz("They ___ in London.", listOf("lives", "live", "living", "is live"), 1),
            Quiz("___ she like tea?", listOf("Do", "Does", "Is", "Are"), 1),
            Quiz("I ___ not drink coffee.", listOf("does", "am", "do", "are"), 2),
            Quiz("Does he ___ English?", listOf("speaks", "speaking", "to speaks", "speak"), 3)
        )
    ),
    GUnit(
        "past-simple", "A2", "Past Simple: что случилось вчера",
        "Past Simple рассказывает о том, что уже закончилось в прошлом. У правильных глаголов добавляем -ed: work — worked. У неправильных глаголов форма особая: go — went, see — saw. Для вопроса и отрицания берём did, а глагол остаётся в начальной форме.",
        "глагол-ed или особая форма • did + глагол (вопрос, отрицание)",
        listOf("I visited my aunt yesterday. — Вчера я навестил тётю.", "We went to the sea last year. — В прошлом году мы ездили на море.", "Did you see him? — Ты видел его?"),
        "Нельзя говорить «did you went». После did глагол не меняется: did you go.",
        listOf(
            Quiz("Yesterday I ___ to the cinema.", listOf("go", "goed", "went", "gone"), 2),
            Quiz("She ___ a letter last week.", listOf("writed", "write", "written", "wrote"), 3),
            Quiz("Did he ___ the door?", listOf("closed", "closes", "close", "closing"), 2),
            Quiz("We ___ not see the film.", listOf("do", "did", "were", "are"), 1),
            Quiz("They ___ football yesterday.", listOf("play", "plays", "playing", "played"), 3)
        )
    ),
    GUnit(
        "present-perfect", "B1", "Present Perfect: have / has + V3",
        "Present Perfect связывает прошлое с настоящим. Он нужен, когда важен результат сейчас или опыт: I have lost my keys — ключей до сих пор нет. Форма: have или has и третья форма глагола. С he, she, it ставим has.",
        "have / has + третья форма глагола (V3)",
        listOf("I have already eaten. — Я уже поел.", "She has never been to Paris. — Она никогда не была в Париже.", "Have you finished? — Ты закончил?"),
        "Нельзя ставить точное время в прошлом: «I have seen him yesterday». Для yesterday нужен Past Simple: I saw him yesterday.",
        listOf(
            Quiz("She ___ never been to Italy.", listOf("have", "has", "is", "did"), 1),
            Quiz("I have ___ my homework.", listOf("do", "did", "doing", "done"), 3),
            Quiz("Have you ___ the news?", listOf("hear", "hearing", "heard", "hears"), 2),
            Quiz("He has lived here ___ 2015.", listOf("for", "since", "from", "during"), 1),
            Quiz("They ___ just arrived.", listOf("has", "did", "have", "are"), 2)
        )
    ),
    GUnit(
        "second-conditional", "B2", "Second Conditional: если бы…",
        "Second Conditional говорит о нереальной или маловероятной ситуации. После if ставим Past Simple, а во второй части — would и глагол без to. Это не рассказ о прошлом: форма Past Simple лишь показывает, что ситуация нереальна. С to be после if часто используют were: If I were you.",
        "If + Past Simple, would + глагол",
        listOf("If I had more time, I would travel. — Если бы у меня было больше времени, я бы путешествовал.", "If she were here, she would help us. — Если бы она была здесь, она бы нам помогла.", "What would you do if you won? — Что бы ты сделал, если бы выиграл?"),
        "Нельзя ставить would после if: «If I would know» — ошибка. Правильно: If I knew.",
        listOf(
            Quiz("If I ___ rich, I would buy a boat.", listOf("am", "will be", "would be", "were"), 3),
            Quiz("If he studied more, he ___ pass.", listOf("will", "can", "would", "did"), 2),
            Quiz("I would call you if I ___ your number.", listOf("have", "would have", "had", "will have"), 2),
            Quiz("What ___ you do if you lost your job?", listOf("will", "did", "do", "would"), 3),
            Quiz("If we ___ more money, we would move.", listOf("have", "would have", "having", "had"), 3)
        )
    ),
    GUnit(
        "inversion", "C1", "Инверсия после отрицательных слов",
        "В формальной и письменной речи после слов с отрицательным смыслом порядок слов меняется: сначала вспомогательный глагол, потом подлежащее. Так делают после never, rarely, seldom, hardly, not only. Это усиливает фразу и звучит книжно: вместо I have never seen — Never have I seen.",
        "Отрицательное слово + вспомогательный глагол + подлежащее + основной глагол",
        listOf("Never have I seen such a view. — Никогда я не видел такого вида.", "Rarely does he complain. — Он редко жалуется.", "Not only did she win, but she also broke the record. — Она не только победила, но и побила рекорд."),
        "После Never здесь нельзя оставлять прямой порядок слов: «Never I have seen» — ошибка.",
        listOf(
            Quiz("Never ___ such a beautiful place.", listOf("I have seen", "I saw", "have I seen", "did I saw"), 2),
            Quiz("Rarely ___ late for work.", listOf("is he", "he is", "he was", "does he be"), 0),
            Quiz("Not only ___ the exam, but she also got the top mark.", listOf("she passed", "did she pass", "she did pass", "passed she"), 1),
            Quiz("Hardly ___ arrived when it started to rain.", listOf("we had", "did we", "had we", "we have"), 2),
            Quiz("Seldom ___ such talent.", listOf("do we see", "we see", "we do see", "see we"), 0)
        )
    ),
    GUnit(
        "mandative", "C2", "Сослагательное после suggest, insist, demand",
        "После глаголов suggest, insist, demand, recommend и слов essential, vital, important в формальной речи используют начальную форму глагола без -s и без would. Даже с he, she, it окончание -s не нужно: I insist that he leave. Так пишут в договорах, приказах и официальных текстах.",
        "suggest / insist / demand + that + подлежащее + начальная форма глагола",
        listOf("I suggest that he apply at once. — Я предлагаю, чтобы он обратился немедленно.", "It is vital that she be present. — Крайне важно, чтобы она присутствовала.", "They demanded that the report be rewritten. — Они потребовали, чтобы отчёт переписали."),
        "В строгой норме не добавляют -s: «I insist that he leaves» считается менее формальным и неточным.",
        listOf(
            Quiz("I insist that he ___ the form today.", listOf("fills", "filled", "will fill", "fill"), 3),
            Quiz("It is essential that she ___ on time.", listOf("arrives", "arrive", "arrived", "has arrived"), 1),
            Quiz("The manager demanded that the report ___ finished by Friday.", listOf("is", "was", "be", "being"), 2),
            Quiz("We recommend that every student ___ the rules.", listOf("reads", "has read", "is reading", "read"), 3),
            Quiz("It is vital that nobody ___ the room.", listOf("leave", "leaves", "left", "leaving"), 0)
        )
    )
)

private val TERMS = listOf(
    Term("Глагол (verb)", "Слово, которое называет действие или состояние: run, think, be. Отвечает на вопрос «что делать?».", "She reads a book. — Она читает книгу. Здесь reads — глагол.", "Verb"),
    Term("Существительное (noun)", "Слово, которое называет предмет, человека, место или идею: book, teacher, city. Отвечает на вопрос «кто? что?».", "The dog is big. — Собака большая. Здесь dog — существительное.", "Noun"),
    Term("Прилагательное (adjective)", "Слово, которое описывает существительное: big, red, happy. Отвечает на вопрос «какой?».", "It is a red car. — Это красная машина. Здесь red — прилагательное.", "Adjective"),
    Term("Подлежащее (subject)", "Тот, кто выполняет действие в предложении. Обычно стоит перед глаголом.", "Tom plays football. — Том играет в футбол. Подлежащее — Tom.", "Subject_(grammar)"),

    Term("Гласные звуки (vowels)", "Звуки, при которых воздух идёт свободно, ничто ему не мешает: a, e, i, o, u. Слог строится вокруг гласного звука.", "cat — гласный звук /æ/", "Vowel"),
    Term("Согласные звуки (consonants)", "Звуки, при которых воздух встречает преграду: губы, зубы или язык. Например: b, t, s, m.", "top — согласные t и p", "Consonant")
)

// ===================== ПОМОЩНИКИ =====================

fun parseCsv(text: String): List<List<String>> {
    val rows = ArrayList<List<String>>()
    var row = ArrayList<String>()
    val sb = StringBuilder()
    var q = false
    val t = text.removePrefix("\uFEFF")
    var i = 0
    while (i < t.length) {
        val c = t[i]
        if (q) {
            if (c == '"') {
                if (i + 1 < t.length && t[i + 1] == '"') {
                    sb.append('"')
                    i++
                } else {
                    q = false
                }
            } else {
                sb.append(c)
            }
        } else if (c == '"') {
            q = true
        } else if (c == ';') {
            row.add(sb.toString())
            sb.setLength(0)
        } else if (c == '\n') {
            row.add(sb.toString())
            sb.setLength(0)
            rows.add(row)
            row = ArrayList()
        } else if (c != '\r') {
            sb.append(c)
        }
        i++
    }
    if (sb.isNotEmpty() || row.isNotEmpty()) {
        row.add(sb.toString())
        rows.add(row)
    }
    return rows
}

fun parseWords(text: String): List<Word> {
    val out = ArrayList<Word>()
    for (r in parseCsv(text)) {
        if (r.size < 9) continue
        val level = r[1].trim().uppercase()
        val w = r[2].trim()
        if (w.isEmpty() || level !in LEVELS) continue
        val tr = r[6].split("|").map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
        if (tr.isEmpty()) continue
        out.add(
            Word(
                w.lowercase(), level, w, r[3].trim(), r[4].trim(), r[5].trim(), tr,
                r[8].trim(), r.getOrElse(9) { "" }.trim(), r.getOrElse(10) { "" }.trim()
            )
        )
    }
    return out
}

fun norm(s: String): String =
    s.lowercase().replace('ё', 'е').filter { it.isLetterOrDigit() || it == ' ' }.trim().replace(Regex("\\s+"), " ")

fun lev(a: String, b: String): Int {
    if (a == b) return 0
    var prev = IntArray(b.length + 1) { it }
    for (i in 1..a.length) {
        val cur = IntArray(b.length + 1)
        cur[0] = i
        for (j in 1..b.length) {
            val cost = if (a[i - 1] == b[j - 1]) 0 else 1
            cur[j] = minOf(prev[j] + 1, cur[j - 1] + 1, prev[j - 1] + cost)
        }
        prev = cur
    }
    return prev[b.length]
}

fun wordAt(s: String, off: Int): String? {
    if (s.isEmpty()) return null
    var a = off.coerceIn(0, s.length - 1)
    var b = a
    if (!(s[a].isLetter() || s[a] == '\'')) return null
    while (a > 0 && (s[a - 1].isLetter() || s[a - 1] == '\'')) a--
    while (b < s.length - 1 && (s[b + 1].isLetter() || s[b + 1] == '\'')) b++
    return s.substring(a, b + 1).lowercase()
}

fun fmt1(x: Float): String = String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", x)

fun daysWord(n: Int): String = if (n % 10 == 1 && n % 100 != 11) "день" else "дней"

fun sectionName(sec: String): String = when (sec) {
    "grammar" -> "Грамматика"
    "vocab" -> "Лексика"
    else -> "Чтение"
}

fun Context.toast(msg: String) {
    Toast.makeText(this, msg, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
}

// ===================== ЛОГИКА =====================

class AppVm(app: Application) : AndroidViewModel(app) {
    private val stateFile = File(app.filesDir, "state.json")
    private val wordsFile = File(app.filesDir, "words.csv")
    private var baseWords: List<Word> = emptyList()

    var words by mutableStateOf<List<Word>>(emptyList())
        private set
    val prog = mutableStateMapOf<String, Prog>()
    val days = mutableStateMapOf<Long, Int>()
    val unitPassed = mutableStateMapOf<String, Boolean>()
    val unitReview = mutableStateMapOf<String, Boolean>()
    val tests = mutableStateListOf<TestRec>()
    val custom = mutableStateListOf<Word>()
    val pinned = mutableStateListOf<String>()
    var level by mutableStateOf("A0")
    var startDone by mutableStateOf(false)
    var limit by mutableStateOf(10)
    var accent by mutableStateOf("UK")
    var nextTest by mutableStateOf(0L)
    var newPaused by mutableStateOf(false)
    var test by mutableStateOf<TestSession?>(null)
    private var pending = 0
    private var lastTouch = System.currentTimeMillis()

    init {
        val csv = runCatching { if (wordsFile.exists()) wordsFile.readText() else SEED_CSV }.getOrDefault(SEED_CSV)
        baseWords = parseWords(csv).ifEmpty { parseWords(SEED_CSV) }
        runCatching { if (stateFile.exists()) load(JSONObject(stateFile.readText())) }
        rebuild()
        updatePause()
    }

    fun today(): Long = LocalDate.now().toEpochDay()

    private fun rebuild() {
        val m = LinkedHashMap<String, Word>()
        for (w in baseWords + custom) m.putIfAbsent(w.key, w)
        words = m.values.sortedBy { LEVELS.indexOf(it.level) }
    }

    // ---------- сохранение ----------

    private fun toJson(): JSONObject {
        val o = JSONObject()
        o.put("level", level)
        o.put("startDone", startDone)
        o.put("limit", limit)
        o.put("accent", accent)
        o.put("nextTest", nextTest)
        o.put("newPaused", newPaused)
        val p = JSONObject()
        for ((k, v) in prog) {
            p.put(k, JSONArray(listOf(v.s, v.due, if (v.mastered) 1 else 0, v.intro, v.lapses, v.lastWrong)))
        }
        o.put("prog", p)
        val d = JSONObject()
        for ((k, v) in days) d.put(k.toString(), v)
        o.put("days", d)
        o.put("unitsPassed", JSONArray(unitPassed.filter { it.value }.keys.toList()))
        o.put("unitsReview", JSONArray(unitReview.filter { it.value }.keys.toList()))
        o.put("tests", JSONArray(tests.map { JSONArray(listOf(it.day, it.kind, it.level, it.percent, if (it.passed) 1 else 0)) }))
        o.put("custom", JSONArray(custom.map { JSONArray(listOf(it.word, it.level, it.tr.joinToString("|"), it.example, it.exampleRu)) }))
        o.put("pinned", JSONArray(pinned.toList()))
        return o
    }

    private fun load(o: JSONObject) {
        level = o.optString("level", "A0").let { if (it in LEVELS) it else "A0" }
        startDone = o.optBoolean("startDone", false)
        limit = o.optInt("limit", 10)
        accent = o.optString("accent", "UK")
        nextTest = o.optLong("nextTest", 0L)
        newPaused = o.optBoolean("newPaused", false)
        prog.clear(); days.clear(); unitPassed.clear(); unitReview.clear()
        tests.clear(); custom.clear(); pinned.clear()
        o.optJSONObject("prog")?.let { p ->
            for (k in p.keys()) {
                val a = p.getJSONArray(k)
                prog[k] = Prog(a.getInt(0), a.getLong(1), a.getInt(2) == 1, a.getLong(3), a.getInt(4), a.getLong(5))
            }
        }
        o.optJSONObject("days")?.let { d ->
            for (k in d.keys()) days[k.toLong()] = d.getInt(k)
        }
        o.optJSONArray("unitsPassed")?.let { a ->
            for (i in 0 until a.length()) unitPassed[a.getString(i)] = true
        }
        o.optJSONArray("unitsReview")?.let { a ->
            for (i in 0 until a.length()) unitReview[a.getString(i)] = true
        }
        o.optJSONArray("tests")?.let { a ->
            for (i in 0 until a.length()) {
                val r = a.getJSONArray(i)
                tests.add(TestRec(r.getLong(0), r.getString(1), r.getString(2), r.getInt(3), r.getInt(4) == 1))
            }
        }
        o.optJSONArray("custom")?.let { a ->
            for (i in 0 until a.length()) {
                val r = a.getJSONArray(i)
                val w = r.getString(0)
                custom.add(Word(w.lowercase(), r.getString(1), w, "", "", "", r.getString(2).split("|"), r.getString(3), r.getString(4), ""))
            }
        }
        o.optJSONArray("pinned")?.let { a ->
            for (i in 0 until a.length()) pinned.add(a.getString(i))
        }
    }

    fun save() {
        val s = toJson().toString()
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) { runCatching { stateFile.writeText(s) } }
    }

    fun exportJson(): String = toJson().toString(2)

    fun importBackup(text: String): Boolean = try {
        load(JSONObject(text))
        rebuild()
        updatePause()
        save()
        true
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        false
    }

    fun importCsv(text: String): Int {
        val parsed = parseWords(text)
        if (parsed.isEmpty()) return 0
        runCatching { wordsFile.writeText(text) }
        baseWords = parsed
        rebuild()
        updatePause()
        return parsed.size
    }

    // ---------- время и стрик ----------

    fun touch() {
        lastTouch = System.currentTimeMillis()
    }

    fun tick(count: Boolean) {
        if (!count || System.currentTimeMillis() - lastTouch > 120_000) return
        pending++
        if (pending >= 10) flush()
    }

    fun flush() {
        if (pending > 0) {
            val d = today()
            days[d] = (days[d] ?: 0) + pending
            pending = 0
            save()
        }
    }

    fun totalSec(): Int = days.values.sum() + pending
    fun hoursLabel(): String = fmt1(totalSec() / 3600f)

    private fun testDays(): Set<Long> = tests.filter { it.passed }.map { it.day }.toSet()
    private fun counted(d: Long, td: Set<Long>): Boolean = (days[d] ?: 0) >= 300 || d in td

    fun streak(): Int {
        val td = testDays()
        var d = today()
        if (!counted(d, td)) d--
        var n = 0
        while (counted(d, td)) {
            n++
            d--
        }
        return n
    }

    fun record(): Int {
        val td = testDays()
        val ds = (days.filter { it.value >= 300 }.keys + td).toSortedSet()
        var best = 0
        var run = 0
        var prev = Long.MIN_VALUE
        for (d in ds) {
            run = if (d == prev + 1) run + 1 else 1
            if (run > best) best = run
            prev = d
        }
        return best
    }

    // ---------- Anki ----------

    fun dueWords(filter: String?): List<Word> {
        val t = today()
        return words.filter { w ->
            (filter == null || w.level == filter) && prog[w.key].let { it != null && !it.mastered && it.due <= t }
        }.sortedBy { prog[it.key]?.due ?: 0L }
    }

    fun introducedToday(): Int {
        val t = today()
        return prog.values.count { it.intro == t }
    }

    fun newLeft(): Int = if (newPaused) 0 else maxOf(0, limit - introducedToday())

    private fun nextNew(filter: String?): Word? {
        for (k in pinned) {
            val w = words.firstOrNull { it.key == k }
            if (w != null && prog[k] == null && (filter == null || w.level == filter)) return w
        }
        return words.firstOrNull { (filter == null || it.level == filter) && prog[it.key] == null }
    }

    fun pickCard(filter: String?): Word? {
        val d = dueWords(filter).firstOrNull()
        if (d != null) return d
        return if (newLeft() > 0) nextNew(filter) else null
    }

    private fun updatePause() {
        val n = dueWords(null).size
        if (n > 50) newPaused = true else if (n < 20) newPaused = false
    }

    private fun checkAnswer(w: Word, input: String): Pair<Boolean, String?> {
        val x = norm(input)
        if (x.isEmpty()) return Pair(false, null)
        var bestD = Int.MAX_VALUE
        var bestT = ""
        for (t in w.tr) {
            val d = lev(x, norm(t))
            if (d < bestD) {
                bestD = d
                bestT = t
            }
        }
        val len = norm(bestT).length
        val allow = if (len >= 8) 2 else if (len >= 5) 1 else 0
        return if (bestD == 0) Pair(true, null) else if (bestD <= allow) Pair(true, bestT) else Pair(false, null)
    }

    private fun applyResult(key: String, ok: Boolean): String {
        val t = today()
        val old = prog[key]
        val intro = old?.intro ?: t
        val lapses = old?.lapses ?: 0
        val lastWrong = old?.lastWrong ?: 0L
        pinned.remove(key)
        val info: String
        if (ok) {
            val s = (old?.s ?: 0) + 1
            if (s >= 6) {
                prog[key] = Prog(6, t, true, intro, lapses, lastWrong)
                info = "Слово закреплено"
            } else {
                prog[key] = Prog(s, t + INTERVALS[s - 1], false, intro, lapses, lastWrong)
                info = "Повторим через ${INTERVALS[s - 1]} дн."
            }
        } else {
            prog[key] = Prog(0, t + 1, false, intro, lapses + 1, t)
            info = "Повторим завтра"
        }
        updatePause()
        save()
        return info
    }

    fun answer(w: Word, input: String?): Res {
        var ok = false
        var fixed: String? = null
        if (input != null) {
            val c = checkAnswer(w, input)
            ok = c.first
            fixed = c.second
        }
        val info = applyResult(w.key, ok)
        return Res(ok, fixed, info)
    }

    fun pin(key: String) {
        if (key !in pinned && prog[key] == null) pinned.add(key)
        save()
    }

    fun addCustom(word: String, tr: String, example: String): Boolean {
        val w = word.trim()
        val t = tr.split("|", ",").map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
        if (w.isEmpty() || t.isEmpty()) return false
        custom.add(Word(w.lowercase(), level, w, "", "", "", t, example.trim(), "", ""))
        rebuild()
        pin(w.lowercase())
        return true
    }

    // ---------- грамматика ----------

    fun finishUnit(id: String, ok: Boolean) {
        if (ok) {
            unitPassed[id] = true
            unitReview.remove(id)
        } else {
            unitReview[id] = true
        }
        save()
    }

    // ---------- тесты ----------

    private fun grammarQs(l: String): List<Q> =
        UNITS.filter { it.level == l }.flatMap { u ->
            u.quiz.map { q -> Q("grammar", l, q.text, q.options, q.answer, "Правило: ${u.formula}", null, u.id) }
        }

    private fun readingQs(l: String): List<Q> =
        TEXTS.filter { it.level == l }.flatMap { t ->
            t.questions.map { q -> Q("reading", l, q.text, q.options, q.answer, "Ответ есть в тексте «${t.title}»", null, null, t.body) }
        }

    private fun vocabQs(l: String, n: Int): List<Q> {
        if (n <= 0) return emptyList()
        val out = ArrayList<Q>()
        for (w in words.filter { it.level == l }.shuffled().take(n)) {
            val right = w.tr.first()
            val near = words.filter { it.level == l && it.key != w.key }.map { it.tr.first() }
                .distinct().filter { it != right }.shuffled()
            val far = words.filter { it.key != w.key }.map { it.tr.first() }
                .distinct().filter { it != right && it !in near }.shuffled()
            val wrong = (near + far).take(3)
            if (wrong.size < 3) continue
            val opts = (wrong + right).shuffled()
            out.add(
                Q("vocab", l, "Что значит слово «${w.word}»?", opts, opts.indexOf(right),
                    "${w.word} — ${w.tr.joinToString(", ")}", w.key, null)
            )
        }
        return out
    }

    fun startStartTest() {
        val qs = ArrayList<Q>()
        for (l in LEVELS.drop(1)) {
            val g = grammarQs(l).shuffled().take(3)
            qs.addAll(g)
            qs.addAll(vocabQs(l, 5 - g.size))
        }
        if (qs.isNotEmpty()) test = TestSession("start", "A1", qs, 0L)
    }

    fun weeklyTarget(): String = LEVELS.getOrElse(LEVELS.indexOf(level) + 1) { "C2" }
    fun weeklyAvailable(): Boolean = startDone && today() >= nextTest

    fun startWeekly() {
        val l = weeklyTarget()
        val qs = grammarQs(l).shuffled().take(20) + vocabQs(l, 25) + readingQs(l)
        if (qs.isNotEmpty()) test = TestSession("weekly", l, qs, 40L * 60L * 1000L)
    }

    fun nextQ() {
        val s = test ?: return
        if (s.result != null) return
        val cur = s.qs[s.index]
        if (s.index + 1 >= s.qs.size) {
            finishTest()
            return
        }
        if (s.kind == "start" && s.qs[s.index + 1].level != cur.level) {
            var a = s.index
            while (a > 0 && s.qs[a - 1].level == cur.level) a--
            var right = 0
            for (k in a..s.index) if (s.answers[k] == s.qs[k].answer) right++
            if (right * 5 < (s.index - a + 1) * 4) {
                finishTest()
                return
            }
        }
        s.index += 1
    }

    fun finishTest() {
        val s = test ?: return
        if (s.result != null) return
        val last = if (s.kind == "start") s.index else s.qs.lastIndex
        val wrong = ArrayList<Pair<Q, Int>>()
        val secTotal = HashMap<String, Int>()
        val secRight = HashMap<String, Int>()
        var right = 0
        for (k in 0..last) {
            val q = s.qs[k]
            secTotal[q.section] = (secTotal[q.section] ?: 0) + 1
            if (s.answers[k] == q.answer) {
                right++
                secRight[q.section] = (secRight[q.section] ?: 0) + 1
            } else {
                wrong.add(Pair(q, s.answers[k]))
            }
        }
        val count = last + 1
        val percent = if (count <= 0) 0 else right * 100 / count
        val secs = listOf("grammar", "vocab", "reading").filter { secTotal.containsKey(it) }
            .map { sec -> Pair(sec, (secRight[sec] ?: 0) * 100 / (secTotal[sec] ?: 1)) }
        val passed: Boolean
        var newLevel: String? = null
        val t = today()
        if (s.kind == "start") {
            var best = "A0"
            for (l in LEVELS.drop(1)) {
                val ks = (0..last).filter { s.qs[it].level == l }
                if (ks.isEmpty()) break
                val r = ks.count { s.answers[it] == s.qs[it].answer }
                if (r * 5 >= ks.size * 4) best = l else break
            }
            level = best
            startDone = true
            passed = true
            newLevel = best
        } else {
            passed = percent >= 75 && secs.all { it.second >= 60 }
            if (passed) {
                level = s.target
                newLevel = s.target
            }
        }
        nextTest = t + 7
        tests.add(TestRec(t, s.kind, s.target, percent, passed))
        for (pair in wrong) {
            val q = pair.first
            val wk = q.wordKey
            if (wk != null) applyResult(wk, false)
            val uid = q.unitId
            if (uid != null) unitReview[uid] = true
        }
        s.result = TestResult(percent, passed, newLevel, secs, wrong)
        updatePause()
        save()
    }

    // ---------- прогресс и медали ----------

    fun levelProgress(l: String): Float {
        val ws = words.filter { it.level == l }
        val us = UNITS.filter { it.level == l }
        val parts = ArrayList<Float>()
        if (ws.isNotEmpty()) parts.add(ws.count { prog[it.key]?.mastered == true }.toFloat() / ws.size)
        if (us.isNotEmpty()) parts.add(us.count { unitPassed[it.id] == true }.toFloat() / us.size)
        return if (parts.isEmpty()) 0f else parts.sum() / parts.size
    }

    fun nextLevel(): String = LEVELS.getOrElse(LEVELS.indexOf(level) + 1) { "C2" }

    fun nextProgressLabel(): String = "К ${nextLevel()}: ${(levelProgress(nextLevel()) * 100).toInt()}%"

    fun nextTestLabel(): String = if (!startDone) "стартовый" else LocalDate.ofEpochDay(nextTest).toString()

    fun weakItems(): List<String> {
        val us = UNITS.filter { unitReview[it.id] == true }.map { it.title }
        val ws = prog.filter { it.value.lapses > 0 && !it.value.mastered && it.value.s <= 1 }
            .entries.sortedByDescending { it.value.lastWrong }.map { it.key }
        return (us + ws).take(10)
    }

    fun streakMedals(): List<Medal> {
        val r = record()
        return listOf(7, 14, 21, 28, 60, 90, 120, 150, 180, 210, 240, 270, 300, 330, 360).map { n ->
            Medal("$n ${daysWord(n)} подряд", "Занимайся $n ${daysWord(n)} без пропусков", r >= n,
                r.toFloat() / n, "${minOf(r, n)} / $n дн.")
        }
    }

    fun timeMedals(): List<Medal> {
        val h = totalSec() / 3600f
        return listOf(1, 10, 20, 50, 100, 200, 500, 1000).map { n ->
            Medal(if (n == 1) "Новичок: 1 час" else "$n часов практики", "Набери $n ч занятий в приложении",
                h >= n, h / n, "${fmt1(minOf(h, n.toFloat()))} / $n ч")
        }
    }

    fun levelMedals(): List<Medal> = LEVELS.drop(1).map { l ->
        val done = LEVELS.indexOf(level) >= LEVELS.indexOf(l)
        Medal("Уровень $l", "Пройди тест на уровень $l", done, if (done) 1f else 0f, if (done) "Получено" else "Не получено")
    }
}

// ===================== АКТИВНОСТЬ =====================

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    private var resumedState by mutableStateOf(false)
    private lateinit var vm: AppVm

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        vm = ViewModelProvider(this)[AppVm::class.java]
        setContent {
            MaterialTheme(colorScheme = lightColorScheme(primary = GreenC, background = BgC, surface = SurfC)) {
                EnglishTrackerRoot(vm, resumedState)
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        super.onResume()
        resumedState = true
    }

    override fun onPause() {
        super.onPause()
        resumedState = false
        vm.flush()
    }
}

// ===================== UI: ОБЩЕЕ =====================

@Composable
fun H(text: String, size: Int = 18, color: Color = InkC) {
    Text(text, fontSize = size.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = color)
}

@Composable
fun Sub(text: String, color: Color = MutedC, size: Int = 14) {
    Text(text, fontSize = size.sp, color = color, lineHeight = (size + 7).sp)
}

@Composable
fun Gap(h: Int) {
    Spacer(Modifier.height(h.dp))
}

@Composable
fun AppCard(modifier: Modifier = Modifier, content: @Composable ColumnScope.() -> Unit) {
    Column(
        modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(22.dp)).background(SurfC)
            .border(1.dp, LineC, RoundedCornerShape(22.dp)).padding(18.dp),
        content = content
    )
}

@Composable
fun Pill(text: String, selected: Boolean, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    Box(
        Modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(50)).background(if (selected) GreenC else ChipC)
            .clickable(onClick = onClick).padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 9.dp)
    ) {
        Text(text, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium, color = if (selected) Color.White else InkC)
    }
}

@Composable
fun Bar(frac: Float, color: Color = GreenC) {
    Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(8.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp)).background(ChipC)) {
        Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(frac.coerceIn(0f, 1f)).fillMaxHeight().background(color))
    }
}

@Composable
fun Tile(label: String, value: String, modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    Column(modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)).background(ChipC).padding(12.dp)) {
        Text(label, fontSize = 12.sp, color = MutedC)
        Gap(4)
        Text(value, fontSize = 16.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = InkC)
    }
}

@Composable
fun Primary(text: String, modifier: Modifier = Modifier, enabled: Boolean = true, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    Button(
        onClick = onClick, enabled = enabled, modifier = modifier, shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = GreenC, contentColor = Color.White),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 18.dp, vertical = 14.dp)
    ) { Text(text, fontSize = 16.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold) }
}

@Composable
fun Secondary(text: String, modifier: Modifier = Modifier, color: Color = InkC, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    OutlinedButton(
        onClick = onClick, modifier = modifier, shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 18.dp, vertical = 14.dp)
    ) { Text(text, fontSize = 16.sp, color = color) }
}

@Composable
fun OptionRow(text: String, bg: Color, selected: Boolean, enabled: Boolean = true, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    Box(
        Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp).fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)).background(bg)
            .border(1.dp, if (selected) GreenC else Color.Transparent, RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
            .clickable(enabled = enabled, onClick = onClick).padding(14.dp)
    ) { Text(text, fontSize = 16.sp, color = InkC) }
}

@Composable
fun LevelFilter(sel: String?, onSel: (String?) -> Unit) {
    Row(Modifier.horizontalScroll(rememberScrollState()), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
        Pill("Все уровни", sel == null) { onSel(null) }
        LEVELS.forEach { l -> Pill(l, sel == l) { onSel(l) } }
    }
}

@Composable
fun StreakChip(n: Int) {
    Row(
        Modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(50)).background(AmberSoftC)
            .border(1.dp, AmberLineC, RoundedCornerShape(50)).padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Icon(Icons.Filled.LocalFireDepartment, contentDescription = null, tint = AmberC, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
        Spacer(Modifier.width(4.dp))
        Text("$n дн.", color = AmberC, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 18.sp)
    }
}

@Composable
fun AppTopBar(vm: AppVm) {
    Row(
        Modifier.fillMaxWidth().background(SurfC).padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text("English", fontSize = 24.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = InkC)
            Text("Уровень ${vm.level}", fontSize = 14.sp, color = MutedC)
        }
        StreakChip(vm.streak())
    }
    Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(1.dp).background(LineC))
}

val TABS: List<Pair<String, ImageVector>> = listOf(
    Pair("Прогресс", Icons.Filled.BarChart),
    Pair("Anki", Icons.Filled.Style),
    Pair("Словарь", Icons.Filled.Book),
    Pair("Грамматика", Icons.Filled.School),
    Pair("Профиль", Icons.Filled.Person)
)

@Composable
fun BottomBar(tab: Int, onSel: (Int) -> Unit) {
    NavigationBar(containerColor = SurfC) {
        TABS.forEachIndexed { i, t ->
            NavigationBarItem(
                selected = tab == i,
                onClick = { onSel(i) },
                icon = { Icon(t.second, contentDescription = null) },
                label = { Text(t.first, fontSize = 11.sp, maxLines = 1) },
                colors = NavigationBarItemDefaults.colors(
                    selectedIconColor = GreenC, selectedTextColor = GreenC, indicatorColor = GreenSoftC,
                    unselectedIconColor = MutedC, unselectedTextColor = MutedC
                )
            )
        }
    }
}

// ===================== UI: КОРЕНЬ =====================

@Composable
fun EnglishTrackerRoot(vm: AppVm, resumed: Boolean) {
    val ctx = LocalContext.current
    var tab by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(0) }
    val tts = remember { TextToSpeech(ctx, null) }
    DisposableEffect(Unit) { onDispose { tts.shutdown() } }
    val speak: (String, Boolean) -> Unit = { text, us ->
        tts.setLanguage(if (us) Locale.US else Locale.UK)
        tts.speak(text, TextToSpeech.QUEUE_FLUSH, null, "w")
        vm.touch()
    }
    // Время считается само: пока приложение открыто, на экране, и были касания в последние 2 минуты.
    LaunchedEffect(resumed, tab) {
        while (resumed) {
            delay(1000)
            vm.tick(tab != 4)
        }
    }
    BackHandler(enabled = vm.test != null) { vm.test = null }
    Column(
        Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(BgC).pointerInput(Unit) {
            awaitPointerEventScope {
                while (true) {
                    awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
                    vm.touch()
                }
            }
        }
    ) {
        AppTopBar(vm)
        Box(Modifier.weight(1f).fillMaxWidth()) {
            val s = vm.test
            if (s != null) {
                TestBody(vm, s)
            } else {
                when (tab) {
                    0 -> ProgressScreen(vm)
                    1 -> AnkiScreen(vm, speak)
                    2 -> DictScreen(vm, speak)
                    3 -> GrammarScreen(vm)
                    else -> ProfileScreen(vm)
                }
            }
        }
        if (vm.test == null) BottomBar(tab) { tab = it }
    }
}

// ===================== UI: ПРОГРЕСС =====================

@Composable
fun ProgressScreen(vm: AppVm) {
    LazyColumn(
        Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentPadding = PaddingValues(16.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
    ) {
        item { TodayCard(vm) }
        item { TestCard(vm) }
        item { H("Прогресс по уровням", 20) }
        items(LEVELS) { l -> LevelCard(vm, l) }
    }
}

@Composable
fun TodayCard(vm: AppVm) {
    val due = vm.dueWords(null).size
    AppCard {
        H("Сегодня")
        Gap(10)
        Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
            Tile("Повторы", "$due", Modifier.weight(1f))
            Tile("Новые слова", "${vm.introducedToday()} из ${vm.limit}", Modifier.weight(1f))
            Tile("Стрик", "${vm.streak()} дн.", Modifier.weight(1f))
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun TestCard(vm: AppVm) {
    AppCard {
        if (!vm.startDone) {
            H("Стартовый тест")
            Gap(6)
            Sub("Определит твой уровень. Вопросы идут от A1 и заканчиваются на первом непройденном уровне.")
            Gap(12)
            Primary("Начать") { vm.startStartTest() }
        } else {
            H("Тест недели")
            Gap(6)
            if (vm.weeklyAvailable()) {
                Sub("Доступен. Проверяет уровень ${vm.weeklyTarget()}, до 40 минут, порог 75%.")
                Gap(12)
                Primary("Начать тест") { vm.startWeekly() }
            } else {
                Sub("Следующий тест: ${vm.nextTestLabel()}")
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun LevelCard(vm: AppVm, l: String) {
    val ws = vm.words.filter { it.level == l }
    val m = ws.count { vm.prog[it.key]?.mastered == true }
    val us = UNITS.filter { it.level == l }
    val up = us.count { vm.unitPassed[it.id] == true }
    val p = vm.levelProgress(l)
    val cur = l == vm.level
    AppCard {
        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Box(Modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)).background(if (cur) GreenSoftC else ChipC).padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 8.dp)) {
                Text(l, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 18.sp, color = if (cur) GreenC else InkC)
            }
            Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
            Text("${(p * 100).toInt()}%", fontSize = 22.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = GreenC)
        }
        Gap(12)
        Bar(p)
        Gap(12)
        Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
            Tile("Слова", "$m из ${ws.size}", Modifier.weight(1f))
            Tile("Грамматика", "$up из ${us.size}", Modifier.weight(1f))
        }
    }
}

// ===================== UI: ANKI =====================

@Composable
fun AnkiScreen(vm: AppVm, speak: (String, Boolean) -> Unit) {
    var filter by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var card by remember { mutableStateOf(vm.pickCard(null)) }
    var input by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var res by remember { mutableStateOf<Res?>(null) }
    LaunchedEffect(filter) {
        card = vm.pickCard(filter)
        res = null
        input = ""
    }
    val due = vm.dueWords(filter).size
    Column(
        Modifier.fillMaxSize().verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()).padding(16.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
    ) {
        LevelFilter(filter) { filter = it }
        Sub("Повторы: $due  •  Новых сегодня осталось: ${vm.newLeft()}")
        val w = card
        if (w == null) {
            AppCard {
                H("На сегодня всё")
                Gap(6)
                Sub("Повторять пока нечего, а лимит новых слов исчерпан. Загляни завтра или измени лимит в профиле.")
            }
        } else {
            AppCard {
                Text(
                    if (w.pos.isNotBlank()) "${w.level} • ${w.pos}" else w.level,
                    fontSize = 13.sp, color = BlueC,
                    modifier = Modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)).background(BlueSoftC).padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                )
                if (w.emoji.isNotBlank()) {
                    Gap(8)
                    Text(w.emoji, fontSize = 56.sp, modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.CenterHorizontally))
                }
                Gap(8)
                Text(
                    w.word, fontSize = 38.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = InkC,
                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                )
                Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    if (w.ipa.isNotBlank()) Text("UK ${w.ipa}", fontSize = 18.sp, color = MutedC)
                    IconButton(onClick = {
                        val us = vm.accent == "US"
                        speak(if (us && w.us.isNotBlank()) w.us else w.word, us)
                    }) { Icon(Icons.Filled.VolumeUp, contentDescription = null, tint = GreenC) }
                }
                if (w.us.isNotBlank()) {
                    Text(
                        "US: ${w.us}  •  UK: ${w.word}", fontSize = 14.sp, color = BlueC,
                        textAlign = TextAlign.Center, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                    )
                }
                Gap(14)
                val r = res
                if (r == null) {
                    OutlinedTextField(
                        value = input, onValueChange = { input = it; vm.touch() }, singleLine = true,
                        label = { Text("Перевод на русский") }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                        keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(imeAction = ImeAction.Done),
                        keyboardActions = KeyboardActions(onDone = { if (input.isNotBlank()) res = vm.answer(w, input) })
                    )
                    Gap(12)
                    Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                        Secondary("Не помню", Modifier.weight(1f), RedC) { res = vm.answer(w, null) }
                        Primary("Проверить", Modifier.weight(1f), input.isNotBlank()) { res = vm.answer(w, input) }
                    }
                } else {
                    Column(
                        Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                            .background(if (r.ok) GreenSoftC else RedSoftC).padding(14.dp)
                    ) {
                        val title = if (r.ok) {
                            if (r.fixed != null) "Верно (проверь написание: ${r.fixed})" else "Верно"
                        } else "Неверно"
                        Text(title, fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = if (r.ok) GreenC else RedC)
                        Gap(6)
                        Text("Перевод: ${w.tr.joinToString(", ")}", fontSize = 16.sp, color = InkC)
                        if (w.example.isNotBlank()) {
                            Gap(8)
                            Text(w.example, fontSize = 15.sp, color = InkC)
                            if (w.exampleRu.isNotBlank()) Text(w.exampleRu, fontSize = 14.sp, color = MutedC)
                        }
                        Gap(8)
                        Text(r.info, fontSize = 13.sp, color = MutedC)
                    }
                    Gap(12)
                    Primary("Далее", Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                        card = vm.pickCard(filter)
                        res = null
                        input = ""
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        Gap(8)
    }
}

// ===================== UI: СЛОВАРЬ =====================

@Composable
fun DictScreen(vm: AppVm, speak: (String, Boolean) -> Unit) {
    val ctx = LocalContext.current
    var q by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var filter by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var showAdd by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val picker = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocument()) { uri: Uri? ->
        if (uri != null) {
            val text = runCatching {
                ctx.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.bufferedReader(Charsets.UTF_8)?.use { it.readText() }
            }.getOrNull()
            val n = if (text == null) 0 else vm.importCsv(text)
            ctx.toast(if (n > 0) "Загружено слов: $n" else "Не удалось прочитать словарь")
        }
    }
    val list = remember(vm.words, q, filter) {
        val s = q.trim().lowercase()
        vm.words.filter { w ->
            (filter == null || w.level == filter) &&
                (s.isEmpty() || w.key.contains(s) || w.tr.any { t -> t.lowercase().contains(s) })
        }
    }
    LazyColumn(
        Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentPadding = PaddingValues(16.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
    ) {
        item {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                    Text("Словарь", fontSize = 24.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = InkC)
                    Sub("Слов: ${vm.words.size}  •  A0–C2")
                }
                IconButton(onClick = { showAdd = true }) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.Add, contentDescription = "Добавить слово", tint = GreenC)
                }
            }
        }
        item {
            Column {
                Secondary("Загрузить словарь (CSV)", Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) { picker.launch(arrayOf("*/*")) }
                Gap(6)
                Sub("Колонки через «;»: id; level; word; pos; ipa; us_word; translations_ru; translations_en; example; example_tr; emoji. Варианты перевода — через «|».", size = 12)
            }
        }
        item {
            OutlinedTextField(
                value = q, onValueChange = { q = it }, singleLine = true, label = { Text("Поиск") },
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
            )
        }
        item { LevelFilter(filter) { filter = it } }
        items(list, key = { it.key }) { w -> WordRow(vm, w, speak) }
    }
    if (showAdd) AddWordDialog(vm, "") { showAdd = false }
}

@Composable
fun WordRow(vm: AppVm, w: Word, speak: (String, Boolean) -> Unit) {
    val p = vm.prog[w.key]
    val st = when {
        p == null -> "Новое"
        p.mastered -> "Закреплено"
        else -> "В повторении"
    }
    AppCard {
        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Text(w.word, fontSize = 22.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = InkC)
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                    Text(
                        w.level, fontSize = 12.sp, color = MutedC,
                        modifier = Modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)).background(ChipC).padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 3.dp)
                    )
                }
                if (w.ipa.isNotBlank()) Sub(w.ipa)
                Text(w.tr.joinToString(", "), fontSize = 16.sp, color = InkC)
            }
            Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.End) {
                IconButton(onClick = {
                    val us = vm.accent == "US"
                    speak(if (us && w.us.isNotBlank()) w.us else w.word, us)
                }) { Icon(Icons.Filled.VolumeUp, contentDescription = null, tint = GreenC) }
                Text(st, fontSize = 12.sp, color = MutedC)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun AddWordDialog(vm: AppVm, preset: String, onClose: () -> Unit) {
    val ctx = LocalContext.current
    var w by remember { mutableStateOf(preset) }
    var tr by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var ex by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onClose,
        title = { Text("Новое слово") },
        text = {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                OutlinedTextField(w, { w = it }, label = { Text("Слово (English)") }, singleLine = true)
                OutlinedTextField(tr, { tr = it }, label = { Text("Перевод (через запятую)") }, singleLine = true)
                OutlinedTextField(ex, { ex = it }, label = { Text("Пример (необязательно)") })
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = {
                if (vm.addCustom(w, tr, ex)) {
                    ctx.toast("Слово добавлено в Anki")
                    onClose()
                }
            }) { Text("Добавить") }
        },
        dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = onClose) { Text("Отмена") } }
    )
}

// ===================== UI: ГРАММАТИКА =====================

@Composable
fun GrammarScreen(vm: AppVm) {
    var mode by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    var unit by remember { mutableStateOf<GUnit?>(null) }
    var text by remember { mutableStateOf<TextItem?>(null) }
    var lvl by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    val u = unit
    val tx = text
    if (u != null) {
        UnitScreen(vm, u) { unit = null }
    } else if (tx != null) {
        TextScreen(vm, tx) { text = null }
    } else {
        val ctx = LocalContext.current
        Column(
            Modifier.fillMaxSize().verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()).padding(16.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
        ) {
            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                Pill("Правила", mode == 0) { mode = 0 }
                Pill("Тексты", mode == 1) { mode = 1 }
                Pill("Термины", mode == 2) { mode = 2 }
            }
            if (mode == 0) {
                LevelFilter(lvl) { lvl = it }
                UNITS.filter { lvl == null || it.level == lvl }.forEach { uu -> UnitRow(vm, uu) { unit = uu } }
            } else if (mode == 1) {
                Sub("Читай и нажимай на незнакомые слова: приложение покажет перевод.")
                TEXTS.forEach { t ->
                    Row(
                        Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(22.dp)).background(SurfC)
                            .border(1.dp, LineC, RoundedCornerShape(22.dp)).clickable { text = t }.padding(18.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(t.title, fontSize = 17.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = InkC)
                            Sub(t.level, size = 13)
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                Sub("Короткие объяснения терминов. Подробнее — в Википедии.")
                TERMS.forEach { t ->
                    AppCard {
                        H(t.title, 17)
                        Gap(6)
                        Text(t.explain, fontSize = 15.sp, color = InkC, lineHeight = 22.sp)
                        Gap(6)
                        Sub(t.example, size = 13)
                        Gap(8)
                        Secondary("Открыть в Википедии", Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                            runCatching {
                                ctx.startActivity(Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse("https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/" + t.wiki)))
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            Gap(8)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun UnitRow(vm: AppVm, u: GUnit, onOpen: () -> Unit) {
    val status = when {
        vm.unitReview[u.id] == true -> "Повторить"
        vm.unitPassed[u.id] == true -> "Пройден"
        else -> "Не начат"
    }
    Row(
        Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(22.dp)).background(SurfC)
            .border(1.dp, LineC, RoundedCornerShape(22.dp)).clickable(onClick = onOpen).padding(18.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(u.title, fontSize = 17.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = InkC)
            Text("${u.level} • $status", fontSize = 13.sp, color = if (status == "Повторить") AmberC else MutedC)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun UnitScreen(vm: AppVm, u: GUnit, onBack: () -> Unit) {
    val picks = remember(u.id) { mutableStateListOf<Int>().also { l -> repeat(u.quiz.size) { l.add(-1) } } }
    var checked by remember(u.id) { mutableStateOf(false) }
    BackHandler(onBack = onBack)
    Column(
        Modifier.fillMaxSize().verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()).padding(16.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
    ) {
        TextButton(onClick = onBack) { Text("← Назад", color = GreenC) }
        Text(u.title, fontSize = 24.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = InkC)
        AppCard {
            H("Правило")
            Gap(6)
            Text(u.rule, fontSize = 16.sp, color = InkC, lineHeight = 24.sp)
        }
        AppCard {
            H("Схема")
            Gap(8)
            Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)).background(GreenSoftC).padding(14.dp)) {
                Text(u.formula, fontSize = 16.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = GreenC)
            }
        }
        AppCard {
            H("Примеры")
            Gap(6)
            u.examples.forEach { ex ->
                Text(ex, fontSize = 15.sp, color = InkC, lineHeight = 22.sp)
                Gap(6)
            }
        }
        AppCard {
            H("Частая ошибка", 18, RedC)
            Gap(6)
            Text(u.mistake, fontSize = 15.sp, color = InkC, lineHeight = 22.sp)
        }
        H("Мини-тест", 20)
        u.quiz.forEachIndexed { qi, q ->
            AppCard {
                Text("${qi + 1}. ${q.text}", fontSize = 17.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = InkC)
                q.options.forEachIndexed { oi, o ->
                    val sel = picks[qi] == oi
                    val bg = when {
                        !checked -> if (sel) GreenSoftC else ChipC
                        oi == q.answer -> GreenSoftC
                        sel -> RedSoftC
                        else -> ChipC
                    }
                    OptionRow(o, bg, sel && !checked, !checked) { picks[qi] = oi }
                }
            }
        }
        val score = u.quiz.indices.count { picks[it] == u.quiz[it].answer }
        if (!checked) {
            Primary("Проверить", Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), picks.all { it >= 0 }) {
                checked = true
                vm.finishUnit(u.id, score >= 4)
            }
        } else {
            AppCard {
                H("Результат: $score из ${u.quiz.size}")
                Gap(4)
                Sub(if (score >= 4) "Юнит пройден." else "Нужно минимум 4. Перечитай правило и попробуй ещё раз.")
            }
            Secondary("Пройти ещё раз", Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                for (i in picks.indices) picks[i] = -1
                checked = false
            }
        }
        Gap(16)
    }
}

@Composable
fun TextScreen(vm: AppVm, t: TextItem, onBack: () -> Unit) {
    var tapped by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    BackHandler(onBack = onBack)
    Column(
        Modifier.fillMaxSize().verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()).padding(16.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
    ) {
        TextButton(onClick = onBack) { Text("← Назад", color = GreenC) }
        Text(t.title, fontSize = 24.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = InkC)
        Sub(t.level)
        AppCard {
            ClickableText(
                text = AnnotatedString(t.body),
                style = TextStyle(fontSize = 19.sp, lineHeight = 30.sp, color = InkC),
                onClick = { off ->
                    vm.touch()
                    tapped = wordAt(t.body, off)
                }
            )
        }
        Sub("Нажми на слово, чтобы увидеть перевод.", size = 13)
    }
    val w = tapped
    if (w != null) WordDialog(vm, w) { tapped = null }
}

@Composable
fun WordDialog(vm: AppVm, w: String, onClose: () -> Unit) {
    val ctx = LocalContext.current
    val cands = listOf(w, w.removeSuffix("s"), w.removeSuffix("es"), w.removeSuffix("ed"), w.removeSuffix("d"), w.removeSuffix("ing"))
    val found = cands.firstNotNullOfOrNull { c -> vm.words.firstOrNull { it.key == c } }
    var tr by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onClose,
        title = { Text(w) },
        text = {
            if (found != null) {
                Column {
                    Text(found.tr.joinToString(", "), fontSize = 18.sp, color = InkC)
                    if (found.ipa.isNotBlank()) Sub(found.ipa)
                }
            } else {
                Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    Text("Этого слова нет в словаре.", fontSize = 15.sp, color = MutedC)
                    OutlinedTextField(tr, { tr = it }, label = { Text("Твой перевод") }, singleLine = true)
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            TextButton(onClick = {
                if (found != null) {
                    vm.pin(found.key)
                    ctx.toast("Слово добавлено в Anki")
                    onClose()
                } else if (vm.addCustom(w, tr, "")) {
                    ctx.toast("Слово добавлено в Anki")
                    onClose()
                }
            }) { Text(if (found != null) "В Anki" else "Добавить") }
        },
        dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = onClose) { Text("Закрыть") } }
    )
}

// ===================== UI: ТЕСТЫ =====================

@Composable
fun TestBody(vm: AppVm, s: TestSession) {
    var now by remember(s) { mutableStateOf(System.currentTimeMillis()) }
    LaunchedEffect(s) {
        while (s.result == null) {
            delay(1000)
            now = System.currentTimeMillis()
            if (s.limitMs > 0 && now - s.startedAt >= s.limitMs) vm.finishTest()
        }
    }
    val r = s.result
    if (r != null) {
        ResultView(vm, s, r)
    } else {
        val q = s.qs[s.index]
        val left: Long = if (s.limitMs > 0) maxOf(0L, s.limitMs - (now - s.startedAt)) / 1000 else -1L
        Column(
            Modifier.fillMaxSize().verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()).padding(16.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
        ) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Text(
                    if (s.kind == "start") "Стартовый тест" else "Тест недели • ${s.target}",
                    fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = InkC, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                )
                if (left >= 0) {
                    Text(
                        "%02d:%02d".format(left / 60, left % 60), fontSize = 20.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = if (left < 300) RedC else GreenC
                    )
                }
            }
            Sub("Вопрос ${s.index + 1} из ${s.qs.size}  •  ${sectionName(q.section)}")
            Bar((s.index + 1).toFloat() / s.qs.size)
            val ps = q.passage
            if (ps != null) {
                AppCard { Text(ps, fontSize = 16.sp, color = InkC, lineHeight = 24.sp) }
            }
            AppCard {
                Text(q.text, fontSize = 20.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = InkC)
                q.options.forEachIndexed { i, o ->
                    val sel = s.answers[s.index] == i
                    OptionRow(o, if (sel) GreenSoftC else ChipC, sel) {
                        vm.touch()
                        s.answers[s.index] = i
                    }
                }
            }
            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                Secondary("Прервать", Modifier.weight(1f)) { vm.test = null }
                Primary(
                    if (s.index + 1 >= s.qs.size) "Завершить" else "Далее", Modifier.weight(1f),
                    s.answers[s.index] >= 0
                ) { vm.nextQ() }
            }
            Gap(16)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun ResultView(vm: AppVm, s: TestSession, r: TestResult) {
    Column(
        Modifier.fillMaxSize().verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()).padding(16.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
    ) {
        AppCard {
            val title = if (r.passed) {
                if (s.kind == "start") "Уровень определён" else "Тест пройден"
            } else "Тест не пройден"
            H(title, 22, if (r.passed) GreenC else RedC)
            Text("${r.percent}%", fontSize = 48.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = InkC)
            r.sections.forEach { sec -> Sub("${sectionName(sec.first)}: ${sec.second}%", size = 15) }
            if (r.newLevel != null) {
                Gap(8)
                H("Твой уровень: ${r.newLevel}", 18, GreenC)
            }
            if (s.kind == "weekly" && !r.passed) {
                Gap(8)
                Sub("Нужно 75% в целом и 60% в каждом разделе. Следующая попытка через неделю.")
            }
        }
        if (r.wrong.isNotEmpty()) {
            H("Разбор ошибок", 20)
            r.wrong.forEach { pair ->
                val q = pair.first
                val a = pair.second
                AppCard {
                    Text(q.text, fontSize = 16.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = InkC)
                    Gap(6)
                    Text("Твой ответ: ${if (a >= 0) q.options[a] else "—"}", fontSize = 15.sp, color = RedC)
                    Text("Верно: ${q.options[q.answer]}", fontSize = 15.sp, color = GreenC)
                    Gap(4)
                    Sub(q.tip, size = 13)
                }
            }
        }
        Primary("Готово", Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) { vm.test = null }
        Gap(16)
    }
}

// ===================== UI: ПРОФИЛЬ =====================

@Composable
fun ActivityGrid(vm: AppVm) {
    val t = vm.today()
    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(5.dp)) {
        for (r in 0 until 4) {
            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(5.dp)) {
                for (c in 0 until 7) {
                    val d = t - (27 - (r * 7 + c))
                    val sec = vm.days[d] ?: 0
                    val min = if (sec <= 0) 0 else maxOf(1, (sec + 30) / 60)
                    val bg = if (sec <= 0) ChipC else if (sec >= 300) GreenMidC else GreenSoftC
                    Box(
                        Modifier.weight(1f).height(28.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)).background(bg),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        if (min > 0) Text("${min}м", fontSize = 12.sp, color = GreenC)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun MedalCard(m: Medal) {
    AppCard {
        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            Box(
                Modifier.size(48.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(if (m.done) AmberSoftC else ChipC),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Icon(
                    if (m.done) Icons.Filled.EmojiEvents else Icons.Filled.Lock, contentDescription = null,
                    tint = if (m.done) AmberC else MutedC
                )
            }
            Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(m.title, fontSize = 17.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = if (m.done) InkC else MutedC)
                Sub(m.desc, size = 13)
                Gap(8)
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Box(Modifier.weight(1f)) { Bar(m.frac, if (m.done) AmberC else GreenC) }
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                    Text(m.label, fontSize = 12.sp, color = MutedC)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun ProfileScreen(vm: AppVm) {
    val ctx = LocalContext.current
    var mt by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) { vm.flush() }
    val exp = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.CreateDocument("application/json")) { uri: Uri? ->
        if (uri != null) {
            vm.flush()
            val ok = runCatching {
                ctx.contentResolver.openOutputStream(uri)?.use { it.write(vm.exportJson().toByteArray()) }
            }.isSuccess
            ctx.toast(if (ok) "Резервная копия сохранена" else "Не удалось сохранить файл")
        }
    }
    val imp = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocument()) { uri: Uri? ->
        if (uri != null) {
            val text = runCatching {
                ctx.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.bufferedReader()?.use { it.readText() }
            }.getOrNull()
            ctx.toast(if (text != null && vm.importBackup(text)) "Прогресс восстановлен" else "Не удалось прочитать копию")
        }
    }
    val medals = when (mt) {
        0 -> vm.streakMedals()
        1 -> vm.timeMedals()
        else -> vm.levelMedals()
    }
    val weak = vm.weakItems()
    LazyColumn(
        Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentPadding = PaddingValues(16.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
    ) {
        item {
            AppCard {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Text("English", fontSize = 24.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = InkC, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                    Box(Modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)).background(GreenSoftC).padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 8.dp)) {
                        Text("Уровень ${vm.level}", color = GreenC, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    }
                }
                Gap(10)
                Sub("Часы: ${vm.hoursLabel()} ч • След. тест: ${vm.nextTestLabel()} • ${vm.nextProgressLabel()}", size = 15)
            }
        }
        item {
            AppCard {
                H("Слабые темы и слова (до 10)", 20)
                Gap(8)
                if (weak.isEmpty()) {
                    Sub("Пока нет ошибок.", size = 15)
                } else {
                    weak.forEach { x ->
                        Text(x, fontSize = 16.sp, color = RedC, lineHeight = 24.sp)
                        Gap(4)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        item {
            AppCard {
                H("Статистика и календарь активности", 20)
                Gap(12)
                Sub("Всего времени: ${vm.hoursLabel()} ч • Стрик: ${vm.streak()} дн. (рекорд: ${vm.record()} дн.)", size = 16)
                Gap(16)
                ActivityGrid(vm)
            }
        }
        item { H("Достижения и медали", 22) }
        item {
            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                Pill("Стрик", mt == 0) { mt = 0 }
                Pill("Время", mt == 1) { mt = 1 }
                Pill("Уровни", mt == 2) { mt = 2 }
            }
        }
        items(medals) { m -> MedalCard(m) }
        item {
            AppCard {
                H("Настройки")
                Gap(10)
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Text("Новых слов в день", fontSize = 16.sp, color = InkC, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                    OutlinedButton(
                        onClick = { vm.limit = (vm.limit - 1).coerceAtLeast(0); vm.save() },
                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(0.dp), modifier = Modifier.size(40.dp)
                    ) { Text("−", fontSize = 18.sp) }
                    Text("${vm.limit}", fontSize = 18.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = InkC, modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 14.dp))
                    OutlinedButton(
                        onClick = { vm.limit = (vm.limit + 1).coerceAtMost(30); vm.save() },
                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(0.dp), modifier = Modifier.size(40.dp)
                    ) { Text("+", fontSize = 18.sp) }
                }
                Gap(12)
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Text("Озвучка", fontSize = 16.sp, color = InkC, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                    Pill("UK", vm.accent == "UK") { vm.accent = "UK"; vm.save() }
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                    Pill("US", vm.accent == "US") { vm.accent = "US"; vm.save() }
                }
                Gap(12)
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                    Secondary("Экспорт", Modifier.weight(1f)) { exp.launch("backup-${LocalDate.now()}.json") }
